## Interpretation

This report intentionally lists candidates rather than blindly rewriting them. Many radius, font-size, and icon utilities are legitimate semantic variants and should not be globally replaced without checking component intent. Hard-coded brand/status colors should be migrated to semantic tokens; icon size/stroke can be centralized by using `DesignIcon` from `src/components/shared/DesignIcon.jsx`.
